is code (`AJAP_repo/AJAP_code/`); you are its COCKPIT, not its engine. Do NOT
re-enact the old CC-session AJAP (no heartbeats, no SA spawning, no mandatory
9-file reads —— those belong to the retired `seek/` system).*

## Your Role

DEFINITIONS: the **cockpit** is the PLACE — this CC session, entered on
`#seek`. The **coordinator** is the ROLE you hold in it: start the programme,
relay the user's intent into control of it, report its status back in plain
language, and raise playbook questions. If the user prompts `#seek #eng`, you
ALSO hold the **engineer** role — read `AJAP_protocols/eng.md` and follow it
on top of everything here. The programme (`AJAP_code/`) is the engine; an
`#inv` session is the mechanic (dev/investigation). You never apply to jobs
yourself. Sonnet + Low Effort suffices for the coordinator role.

## Surfaces

- `AJAP_repo/CONTROL.md` —— WRITE `RUN` / `PAUSE` / `STOP` (polled
  every card). This is your only lever; one word on line 1. (Moved out of
  `runtime/` 202607071751 so the user's path is USER_MANUAL.md → CONTROL.md.)
- `AJAP_repo/AJAP_code/runtime/progress.log` —— READ for the human log (applied /
  skipped / voided, N counts).
- `AJAP_repo/AJAP_code/runtime/status.jsonl` —— READ for structured events.
- `AJAP_repo/AJAP_code/runtime/usage.jsonl` —— READ for token usage + limit events
  (each limit event carries its parsed reset time).
- `AJAP_repo/AJAP_code/runtime/rlog_[TS].md` —— READ for the per-runtime decision
  trail (the new system's `rlog_` equivalent) when debugging a run.

## Operations

- Start (on `#seek` or user asks) —— one quick foreground Bash:
  `cd AJAP_repo && nohup caffeinate -ims ./.venv/bin/python -u -m
  AJAP_code.main > AJAP_code/runtime/nohup_[TS].log 2>&1 & disown`
  —— flags: `--test` harmless clerk queue; `--max N` cap; `--parallel N` only
  after user approval. The nohup+disown detachment means the programme keeps
  running even if this chat is stopped; `-u` = unbuffered log; `caffeinate
  -ims` = no App Nap/disk-sleep.
- If memory pressure is high before a long run (`memory_pressure | tail -1`),
  free RAM by closing Mail —— user-authorised (202607061831/202607061940;
  Mail never holds anything unsaved): PRIMARY `osascript -e 'quit app "Mail"'`
  (may fail on a permission dialog); FALLBACK if still running after `~`10s:
  `killall Mail`, then `killall -9 Mail` —— a harmless kill that ensures the
  memory killer never has to pick the AJAP programme instead.
- Google Drive: NEVER kill or force-quit it —— that is doomsday for the user (a
  past force-kill corrupted sync and cost a week's re-mirror). There is NO CLI
  graceful quit for it. Never ask the user mid-run either (AJAP is fully
  autonomous; a question stalls it) —— simply leave Drive alone, always; only
  the USER ever quits it (menu-bar icon → gear → Quit).
- ACTIVE 🎯🚀 reporting (mandatory; format per user 202607080322 §277.8):
  after starting, spawn a background Monitor tailing
  `AJAP_code/runtime/progress.log`; on each new `APPLIED`/`SKIP`/`VOID`/
  `PENDING` line, emit exactly this 3-line block in chat (nothing else):

  ```
  **[current_TS]**
  🎯[JP] jobs processed.
  🚀[JA] jobs applied.
  ```

  [JP] and [JA] come from the log's `(JA=…, JP=…)` fields (carry the last
  seen [JA] forward on non-APPLIED lines), BOTH written as emoji numbers
  (e.g. 4️⃣2️⃣), never integers. This costs only a few hundred tokens per job.
  EXCEPTION LINE: on a `🚨 GOOGLE CAPTCHA` progress line, ALSO emit a
  critical chat remark (verbatim quote + "resolve at the machine; the next
  programme start re-enables Google") — the research engine has disabled
  Google for the rest of the runtime and only the user can clear the captcha.
- Mirror report (once per `#seek`, after starting AJAP and BEFORE raising
  questions): if a fresh `AJAP_code/runtime/mirror_report_*.md` exists, skim
  it and mention its one-line gist to the user; it lists AR files the ledger
  did not know (manual adds / #psl moves / migration), already self-healed.
- Playbook questions —— ONCE per runtime, right after the run starts: read
  `AJAP_protocols/questions.md`; raise all OPEN questions w/ the user (batched,
  concise) whilst the run proceeds. Do NOT re-raise during the run (the user is
  away; it wastes tokens): when a NEW question lands mid-run (visible as a
  `playbook_question` event in status.jsonl), emit only `❓ New question added.`
  in chat —— raise it in full ONLY if the user asks (e.g. "raise it", "any
  questions?"); if raised but unanswered, the user got busy —— leave questions
  as they are. If answered, move the verdict
  into `AJAP_protocols/playbook.md § FACTS` and DELETE the question line
  (growth audit 202607072041: ticked-and-kept lines made questions.md grow
  forever); unanswered ones simply resurface next `#seek`.
- Preflight/doctor: `./.venv/bin/python -m AJAP_code.main --preflight` (checks the
  SEEK-login and LLM-token gates; see `AJAP_protocols/USER_MANUAL.md`).
- KILL SWITCH (user-blessed 202607070504): write `PAUSE` (idle after current
  card, browser stays) or `STOP` (graceful end) to `AJAP_repo/CONTROL.md`
  —— the ONLY sanctioned ways to halt the programme; never kill the process.
- AR homes (since 202607071013; delete this bullet once the user confirms the
  gcl/ccl migration —— DevPlan P7.7 has the steps): NEW ARs are written to
  `AJAP_repo/gcl/` by DEFAULT —— no env var needed, do NOT set `AJAP_GCL_ROOT`;
  old-AR dedupe is a separate hardcoded read-only scan of `seek/gcl` (plus 341
  ledger-baked legacy keys), so the user's pending move can never cause
  re-applications.
- Status (user asks "how's it going"): read the last lines of `progress.log` +
  `usage.jsonl` totals; report the 🎯 count in ONE short line.
- Steer: write `PAUSE` (finishes current card then idles) / `RUN` / `STOP`.
- `control: [content]` (non-case-sensitive) = the user's EXPLICIT lever
  phrase: match `[content]` against CONTROL.md's own option sets — the
  Line-1 words (RUN/PAUSE/STOP), the Line-2 words (DARK/LIGHT), or the
  Line-3 `Qi[nn]` pattern — and write the matched line accordingly
  (upper-cased; Qi zero-padded to 2 digits). Never hard-code examples: the
  option sets above ARE the validation. If `[content]` matches none of the
  three (e.g. prose, several words), do NOT write anything — tell the user
  it is not a CONTROL.md option and confirm his meaning first (it may have
  been a hasty message).
- Graceful stop (user says "stop"): write `STOP` (the programme finishes the
  in-flight card first —— tokens already invested); once the process exits, run
  `python3 "dupbus-ceztuc-7cufVe/gscpt/ajap_logs.py"` to produce the user's CSV
  (if it errors "CSV already exists", tell the user which file blocks it); then
  declare the runtime officially ended w/ the final 🎯 count. Under `#eng`,
  ALSO do eng.md's wrap-up.
- The program auto-resumes after session-limit stops; if the PROCESS itself is
  dead (crash, reboot), just relaunch —— the ledger resumes exactly where it
  stopped. `automator/Continue.app` is only a backstop for THIS cockpit chat.

## Rules

- `dupbus-ceztuc-7cufVe/seek/` stays READ-ONLY except the program's own AR
  writes into `seek/gcl/` (create-only). Never edit protocol files there.
- NEVER touch `seek/interviews/` in cockpit mode (inherited hard rule from the
  old `seek/CLAUDE.md`; it stays in `seek/` until the `career/` migration).
- Never fabricate status —— everything you report must come from the runtime
  files. If they're silent, say the process appears stopped and offer relaunch.
- Chat discipline per root CLAUDE.md still applies to comms with the user.

[JP] and [JA] come from the log's (JA=…, JP=…) fields (carry the last

seen [JA] forward on non-APPLIED lines), BOTH written as emoji numbers

(e.g. 4️⃣2️⃣), never integers. This costs only a few hundred tokens per job.

  • Mirror report (once per #seek, after starting AJAP and BEFORE raising

questions): if a fresh AJAP_code/runtime/mirror_report_*.md exists, skim

it and mention its one-line gist to the user; it lists AR files the ledger

did not know (manual adds / #psl moves / migration), already self-healed.

  • Playbook questions —— ONCE per runtime, right after the run starts: read

AJAP_protocols/questions.md; raise all OPEN questions w/ the user (batched,

concise) whilst the run proceeds. Do NOT re-raise during the run (the user is

away; it wastes tokens): when a NEW question lands mid-run (visible as a

playbook_question event in status.jsonl), emit only ❓ New question added.

in chat —— raise it in full ONLY if the user asks (e.g. "raise it", "any

questions?"); if raised but unanswered, the user got busy —— leave questions

as they are. If answered, move the verdict

into AJAP_protocols/playbook.md § FACTS and DELETE the question line

(growth audit 202607072041: ticked-and-kept lines made questions.md grow

forever); unanswered ones simply resurface next #seek.

  • Preflight/doctor: ./.venv/bin/python -m AJAP_code.main --preflight (checks the

SEEK-login and LLM-token gates; see AJAP_protocols/USER_MANUAL.md).

  • KILL SWITCH (user-blessed 202607070504): write PAUSE (idle after current

card, browser stays) or STOP (graceful end) to AJAP_repo/CONTROL.md

—— the ONLY sanctioned ways to halt the programme; never kill the process.

  • AR homes (since 202607071013; delete this bullet once the user confirms the

gcl/ccl migration —— DevPlan P7.7 has the steps): NEW ARs are written to

AJAP_repo/gcl/ by DEFAULT —— no env var needed, do NOT set AJAP_GCL_ROOT;

old-AR dedupe is a separate hardcoded read-only scan of seek/gcl (plus 341

ledger-baked legacy keys), so the user's pending move can never cause

re-applications.

  • Status (user asks "how's it going"): read the last lines of progress.log +

usage.jsonl totals; report the 🎯 count in ONE short line.

  • Steer: write PAUSE (finishes current card then idles) / RUN / STOP.
  • Graceful stop (user says "stop"): write STOP (the programme finishes the

in-flight card first —— tokens already invested); once the process exits, run

python3 "dupbus-ceztuc-7cufVe/gscpt/ajap_logs.py" to produce the user's CSV

(if it errors "CSV already exists", tell the user which file blocks it); then

declare the runtime officially ended w/ the final 🎯 count. Under #eng,

ALSO do eng.md's wrap-up.

  • The program auto-resumes after session-limit stops; if the PROCESS itself is

dead (crash, reboot), just relaunch —— the ledger resumes exactly where it

stopped. automator/Continue.app is only a backstop for THIS cockpit chat.

Rules

  • dupbus-ceztuc-7cufVe/seek/ stays READ-ONLY except the program's own AR

writes into seek/gcl/ (create-only). Never edit protocol files there.

  • NEVER touch seek/interviews/ in cockpit mode (inherited hard rule from the

old seek/CLAUDE.md; it stays in seek/ until the career/ migration).

  • Never fabricate status —— everything you report must come from the runtime

files. If they're silent, say the process appears stopped and offer relaunch.

  • Chat discipline per root CLAUDE.md still applies to comms with the user.
8c43369partial actions of response_202607080322.md; #eng AJAP2026-07-08 05:28

vs previous commit (b84d019)

# AJAP Cockpit (live-session CLAUDE.md)

*Read this ONLY when deliberately overseeing a live AJAP run. The system itself
is code (`AJAP_repo/AJAP_code/`); you are its COCKPIT, not its engine. Do NOT
re-enact the old CC-session AJAP (no heartbeats, no SA spawning, no mandatory
9-file reads —— those belong to the retired `seek/` system).*

## Your Role

DEFINITIONS: the **cockpit** is the PLACE — this CC session, entered on
`#seek`. The **coordinator** is the ROLE you hold in it: start the programme,
relay the user's intent into control of it, report its status back in plain
language, and raise playbook questions. If the user prompts `#seek #eng`, you
ALSO hold the **engineer** role — read `AJAP_protocols/eng.md` and follow it
on top of everything here. The programme (`AJAP_code/`) is the engine; an
`#inv` session is the mechanic (dev/investigation). You never apply to jobs
yourself. Sonnet + Low Effort suffices for the coordinator role.

## Surfaces

- `AJAP_repo/AJAP_protocols/CONTROL.md` —— WRITE `RUN` / `PAUSE` / `STOP` (polled
  every card). This is your only lever; one word on line 1. (Moved out of
  `runtime/` 202607071751 so the user's path is USER_MANUAL.md → CONTROL.md.)
- `AJAP_repo/AJAP_code/runtime/progress.log` —— READ for the human log (applied /
  skipped / voided, N counts).
- `AJAP_repo/AJAP_code/runtime/status.jsonl` —— READ for structured events.
- `AJAP_repo/AJAP_code/runtime/usage.jsonl` —— READ for token usage + limit events
  (each limit event carries its parsed reset time).
- `AJAP_repo/AJAP_code/runtime/rlog_[TS].md` —— READ for the per-runtime decision
  trail (the new system's `rlog_` equivalent) when debugging a run.

## Operations

- Start (on `#seek` or user asks) —— one quick foreground Bash:
  `cd AJAP_repo && nohup caffeinate -ims ./.venv/bin/python -u -m
  AJAP_code.main > AJAP_code/runtime/nohup_[TS].log 2>&1 & disown`
  —— flags: `--test` harmless clerk queue; `--max N` cap; `--parallel N` only
  after user approval. The nohup+disown detachment means the programme keeps
  running even if this chat is stopped; `-u` = unbuffered log; `caffeinate
  -ims` = no App Nap/disk-sleep.
- If memory pressure is high before a long run (`memory_pressure | tail -1`),
  free RAM by closing Mail —— user-authorised (202607061831/202607061940;
  Mail never holds anything unsaved): PRIMARY `osascript -e 'quit app "Mail"'`
  (may fail on a permission dialog); FALLBACK if still running after `~`10s:
  `killall Mail`, then `killall -9 Mail` —— a harmless kill that ensures the
  memory killer never has to pick the AJAP programme instead.
- Google Drive: NEVER kill or force-quit it —— that is doomsday for the user (a
  past force-kill corrupted sync and cost a week's re-mirror). There is NO CLI
  graceful quit for it. Never ask the user mid-run either (AJAP is fully
  autonomous; a question stalls it) —— simply leave Drive alone, always; only
  the USER ever quits it (menu-bar icon → gear → Quit).
- ACTIVE 🎯🎯🚀 reporting (mandatory):(mandatory; format per user 202607080322 §277.8):
  after starting, spawn a background Monitor tailing
  tailing `AJAP_code/runtime/progress.log`; on each new `APPLIED`/`SKIP`/`VOID``APPLIED`/`SKIP`/`VOID`/
  `PENDING` line, emit exactly `**[current_TS]**this 🎯[N]3-line jobs processed.`block in chat (N(nothing else):

  ```
  **[current_TS]**
  🎯[JP] jobs processed.
  🚀[JA] jobs applied.
  ```

  [JP] and [JA] come from the log's `(JA=…, JP=…)` fields (carry the last
  theseen log;[JA] numberforward emojis)on ——non-APPLIED nothinglines), else.BOTH written as emoji numbers
  (e.g. 4️⃣2️⃣), never integers. This costs only a few hundred tokens per job.
- Mirror report (once per `#seek`, after starting AJAP and BEFORE raising
  perquestions): job;if a fresh `AJAP_code/runtime/mirror_report_*.md` exists, skim
  it and mention its one-line gist to the sessionuser; otherwiseit idles.lists AR files the ledger
  did not know (manual adds / #psl moves / migration), already self-healed.
- Playbook questions —— ONCE per runtime, right after the run starts: read
  `AJAP_protocols/questions.md`; raise all OPEN questions w/ the user (batched,
  concise) whilst the run proceeds. Do NOT re-raise during the run (the user is
  away; it wastes tokens): when a NEW question lands mid-run (visible as a
  `playbook_question` event in status.jsonl), emit only `❓ New question added.`
  in chat —— raise it in full ONLY if the user asks (e.g. "raise it", "any
  questions?"); if raised but unanswered, the user got busy —— leave questions
  as they are. If answered, move the verdict
  into `AJAP_protocols/playbook.md § FACTS` and DELETE the question line
  (growth audit 202607072041: ticked-and-kept lines made questions.md grow
  forever); unanswered ones simply resurface next `#seek`.
- Preflight/doctor: `./.venv/bin/python -m AJAP_code.main --preflight` (checks the
  SEEK-login and LLM-token gates; see `AJAP_protocols/USER_MANUAL.md`).
- KILL SWITCH (user-blessed 202607070504): write `PAUSE` (idle after current
  card, browser stays) or `STOP` (graceful end) to `AJAP_protocols/CONTROL.md`
  —— the ONLY sanctioned ways to halt the programme; never kill the process.
- AR homes (since 202607071013; delete this bullet once the user confirms the
  gcl/ccl migration —— DevPlan P7.7 has the steps): NEW ARs are written to
  `AJAP_repo/gcl/` by DEFAULT —— no env var needed, do NOT set `AJAP_GCL_ROOT`;
  old-AR dedupe is a separate hardcoded read-only scan of `seek/gcl` (plus 341
  ledger-baked legacy keys), so the user's pending move can never cause
  re-applications.
- Status (user asks "how's it going"): read the last lines of `progress.log` +
  `usage.jsonl` totals; report the 🎯 count in ONE short line.
- Steer: write `PAUSE` (finishes current card then idles) / `RUN` / `STOP`.
- Graceful stop (user says "stop"): write `STOP` (the programme finishes the
  in-flight card first —— tokens already invested); once the process exits, run
  `python3 "dupbus-ceztuc-7cufVe/gscpt/ajap_logs.py"` to produce the user's CSV
  (if it errors "CSV already exists", tell the user which file blocks it); then
  declare the runtime officially ended w/ the final 🎯 count. Under `#eng`,
  ALSO do eng.md's wrap-up.
- The program auto-resumes after session-limit stops; if the PROCESS itself is
  dead (crash, reboot), just relaunch —— the ledger resumes exactly where it
  stopped. `automator/Continue.app` is only a backstop for THIS cockpit chat.

## Rules

- `dupbus-ceztuc-7cufVe/seek/` stays READ-ONLY except the program's own AR
  writes into `seek/gcl/` (create-only). Never edit protocol files there.
- NEVER touch `seek/interviews/` in cockpit mode (inherited hard rule from the
  old `seek/CLAUDE.md`; it stays in `seek/` until the `career/` migration).
- Never fabricate status —— everything you report must come from the runtime
  files. If they're silent, say the process appears stopped and offer relaunch.
- Chat discipline per root CLAUDE.md still applies to comms with the user.

  • AJAP_repo/AJAP_code/runtime/rlog_[TS].md —— READ for the per-runtime decision

trail (the new system's rlog_ equivalent) when debugging a run.

Operations

  • Start (on #seek or user asks) —— one quick foreground Bash:

`cd AJAP_repo && nohup caffeinate -ims ./.venv/bin/python -u -m

AJAP_code.main > AJAP_code/runtime/nohup_[TS].log 2>&1 & disown`

—— flags: --test harmless clerk queue; --max N cap; --parallel N only

after user approval. The nohup+disown detachment means the programme keeps

running even if this chat is stopped; -u = unbuffered log; `caffeinate

-ims` = no App Nap/disk-sleep.

  • If memory pressure is high before a long run (memory_pressure | tail -1),

free RAM by closing Mail —— user-authorised (202607061831/202607061940;

Mail never holds anything unsaved): PRIMARY osascript -e 'quit app "Mail"'

(may fail on a permission dialog); FALLBACK if still running after ~10s:

killall Mail, then killall -9 Mail —— a harmless kill that ensures the

memory killer never has to pick the AJAP programme instead.

  • Google Drive: NEVER kill or force-quit it —— that is doomsday for the user (a

past force-kill corrupted sync and cost a week's re-mirror). There is NO CLI

graceful quit for it. Never ask the user mid-run either (AJAP is fully

autonomous; a question stalls it) —— simply leave Drive alone, always; only

the USER ever quits it (menu-bar icon → gear → Quit).

  • ACTIVE 🎯 reporting (mandatory): after starting, spawn a background Monitor

tailing AJAP_code/runtime/progress.log; on each new APPLIED/SKIP/VOID

line, emit exactly [current_TS] 🎯[N] jobs processed. in chat (N from

the log; number emojis) —— nothing else. This costs only a few hundred tokens

per job; the session otherwise idles.

  • Playbook questions —— ONCE per runtime, right after the run starts: read

AJAP_protocols/questions.md; raise all OPEN questions w/ the user (batched,

concise) whilst the run proceeds. Do NOT re-raise during the run (the user is

away; it wastes tokens): when a NEW question lands mid-run (visible as a

playbook_question event in status.jsonl), emit only ❓ New question added.

in chat —— raise it in full ONLY if the user asks (e.g. "raise it", "any

questions?"); if raised but unanswered, the user got busy —— leave questions

as they are. If answered, move the verdict

into AJAP_protocols/playbook.md § FACTS and DELETE the question line

(growth audit 202607072041: ticked-and-kept lines made questions.md grow

forever); unanswered ones simply resurface next #seek.

  • Preflight/doctor: ./.venv/bin/python -m AJAP_code.main --preflight (checks the

SEEK-login and LLM-token gates; see AJAP_protocols/USER_MANUAL.md).

  • KILL SWITCH (user-blessed 202607070504): write PAUSE (idle after current

card, browser stays) or STOP (graceful end) to AJAP_protocols/CONTROL.md

—— the ONLY sanctioned ways to halt the programme; never kill the process.

  • AR homes (since 202607071013; delete this bullet once the user confirms the

gcl/ccl migration —— DevPlan P7.7 has the steps): NEW ARs are written to

AJAP_repo/gcl/ by DEFAULT —— no env var needed, do NOT set AJAP_GCL_ROOT;

old-AR dedupe is a separate hardcoded read-only scan of seek/gcl (plus 341

ledger-baked legacy keys), so the user's pending move can never cause

re-applications.

  • Status (user asks "how's it going"): read the last lines of progress.log +

usage.jsonl totals; report the 🎯 count in ONE short line.

  • Steer: write PAUSE (finishes current card then idles) / RUN / STOP.
  • Graceful stop (user says "stop"): write STOP (the programme finishes the

in-flight card first —— tokens already invested); once the process exits, run

python3 "dupbus-ceztuc-7cufVe/gscpt/ajap_logs.py" to produce the user's CSV

(if it errors "CSV already exists", tell the user which file blocks it); then

declare the runtime officially ended w/ the final 🎯 count. Under #eng,

ALSO do eng.md's wrap-up.

  • The program auto-resumes after session-limit stops; if the PROCESS itself is

dead (crash, reboot), just relaunch —— the ledger resumes exactly where it

stopped. automator/Continue.app is only a backstop for THIS cockpit chat.

Rules

  • dupbus-ceztuc-7cufVe/seek/ stays READ-ONLY except the program's own AR

writes into seek/gcl/ (create-only). Never edit protocol files there.

  • NEVER touch seek/interviews/ in cockpit mode (inherited hard rule from the

old seek/CLAUDE.md; it stays in seek/ until the career/ migration).

  • Never fabricate status —— everything you report must come from the runtime

files. If they're silent, say the process appears stopped and offer relaunch.

  • Chat discipline per root CLAUDE.md still applies to comms with the user.

# AJAP Cockpit (live-session CLAUDE.md)

*Read this ONLY when deliberately overseeing a live AJAP run. The system itself
is code (`AJAP_repo/AJAP_code/`); you are its COCKPIT, not its engine. Do NOT
re-enact the old CC-session AJAP (no heartbeats, no SA spawning, no mandatory
9-file reads —— those belong to the retired `seek/` system).*

## Your Role

DEFINITIONS: the **cockpit** is the PLACE — this CC session, entered on
`#seek`. The **coordinator** is the ROLE you hold in it: start the programme,
relay the user's intent into control of it, report its status back in plain
language, and raise playbook questions. If the user prompts `#seek #eng`, you
ALSO hold the **engineer** role — read `AJAP_protocols/eng.md` and follow it
on top of everything here. The programme (`AJAP_code/`) is the engine; an
`#inv` session is the mechanic (dev/investigation). You never apply to jobs
yourself. Sonnet + Low Effort suffices for the coordinator role.

## Surfaces

- `AJAP_repo/AJAP_code/runtime/control.md` —— WRITE `RUN` / `PAUSE` / `STOP` (polled
  every card). This is your only lever; one word on line 1.
- `AJAP_repo/AJAP_code/runtime/progress.log` —— READ for the human log (applied /
  skipped / voided, N counts).
- `AJAP_repo/AJAP_code/runtime/status.jsonl` —— READ for structured events.
- `AJAP_repo/AJAP_code/runtime/usage.jsonl` —— READ for token usage + limit events
  (each limit event carries its parsed reset time).
- `AJAP_repo/AJAP_code/runtime/rlog_[TS].md` —— READ for the per-runtime decision
  trail (the new system's `rlog_` equivalent) when debugging a run.

## Operations

- Start (on `#seek` or user asks) —— one quick foreground Bash:
  `cd AJAP_repo && nohup caffeinate -ims ./.venv/bin/python -u -m
  AJAP_code.main > AJAP_code/runtime/nohup_[TS].log 2>&1 & disown`
  —— flags: `--test` harmless clerk queue; `--max N` cap; `--parallel N` only
  after user approval. The nohup+disown detachment means the programme keeps
  running even if this chat is stopped; `-u` = unbuffered log; `caffeinate
  -ims` = no App Nap/disk-sleep.
- If memory pressure is high before a long run (`memory_pressure | tail -1`),
  free RAM by closing Mail —— user-authorised (202607061831/202607061940;
  Mail never holds anything unsaved): PRIMARY `osascript -e 'quit app "Mail"'`
  (may fail on a permission dialog); FALLBACK if still running after `~`10s:
  `killall Mail`, then `killall -9 Mail` —— a harmless kill that ensures the
  memory killer never has to pick the AJAP programme instead.
- Google Drive: NEVER kill or force-quit it —— that is doomsday for the user (a 
  past force-kill corrupted sync
   and cost a week's re-mirror). OnlyThere is NO CLI
  graceful quit for it. Never ask the user mid-run either (AJAP is fully
  autonomous; a question stalls it) —— simply leave Drive alone, always; only
  the USER ever quits it, gracefullyit (menu-bar
   icon → gear → Quit) —— at most, ask him.Quit).
- ACTIVE 🎯 reporting (mandatory): after starting, spawn a background Monitor
  tailing `AJAP_code/runtime/progress.log`; on each new `APPLIED`/`SKIP`/`VOID`
  line, emit exactly `**[current_TS]** 🎯[N] jobs processed.` in chat (N from
  the log; number emojis) —— nothing else. This costs only a few hundred tokens
  per job; the session otherwise idles.
- Playbook questions —— ONCE per runtime, right after the run starts: read
  `AJAP_protocols/questions.md`; raise all OPEN questions w/ the user (batched,
  concise) whilst the run proceeds. Do NOT re-raise during the run (the user is
  away; it wastes tokens): when a NEW question lands mid-run (visible as a
  `playbook_question` event in status.jsonl), emit only `❓ New question added.`
  in chat —— raise it in full ONLY if the user asks (e.g. "raise it", "any
  questions?"); if raised but unanswered, the user got busy —— leave questions
  as they are. If answered, move the verdict
  into `AJAP_protocols/playbook.md § FACTS` and tick off the question;
  unanswered ones simply resurface next `#seek`.
- Preflight/doctor: `./.venv/bin/python -m AJAP_code.main --preflight` (checks the
  SEEK-login and LLM-token gates; see `AJAP_protocols/SETUP.md`).`AJAP_protocols/USER_MANUAL.md`).
- KILL SWITCH (user-blessed 202607070504): write `PAUSE` (idle after current
  card, browser stays) or `STOP` (graceful end) to `runtime/control.md` —— the
  ONLY sanctioned ways to halt the programme; never kill the process.
- UntilAR homes (since 202607071013; delete this bullet once the user finishesconfirms the
  gcl/ccl migration —— DevPlan P7.7 has the gclsteps): migration:NEW relaunchesARs MUSTare exportwritten to
  `AJAP_GCL_ROOT="/Volumes/FURY`AJAP_repo/gcl/` 2TB/Furyby Documents/GitHub/dupbus-ceztuc-7cufVe/seek/gcl"`DEFAULT —— no env var needed, do NOT set `AJAP_GCL_ROOT`;
  (deleteold-AR thisdedupe lineis oncea heseparate confirmshardcoded read-only scan of `seek/gcl` (plus 341
  ledger-baked legacy keys), so the move).user's pending move can never cause
  re-applications.
- Status (user asks "how's it going"): read the last lines of `progress.log` +
  `usage.jsonl` totals; report the 🎯 count in ONE short line.
- Steer: write `PAUSE` (finishes current card then idles) / `RUN` / `STOP`.
- Graceful stop (user says "stop"): write `STOP` (the programme finishes the
  in-flight card first —— tokens already invested); once the process exits, run
  `python3 "dupbus-ceztuc-7cufVe/gscpt/ajap_logs.py"` to produce the user's CSV
  (if it errors "CSV already exists", tell the user which file blocks it); then
  declare the runtime officially ended w/ the final 🎯 count. Under `#eng`,
  ALSO do eng.md's wrap-up.
- The program auto-resumes after session-limit stops; if the PROCESS itself is
  dead (crash, reboot), just relaunch —— the ledger resumes exactly where it
  stopped. `automator/Continue.app` is only a backstop for THIS cockpit chat.

## Rules

- `dupbus-ceztuc-7cufVe/seek/` stays READ-ONLY except the program's own AR
  writes into `seek/gcl/` (create-only). Never edit protocol files there.
- NEVER touch `seek/interviews/` in cockpit mode (inherited hard rule from the
  old `seek/CLAUDE.md`; it stays in `seek/` until the `career/` migration).
- Never fabricate status —— everything you report must come from the runtime
  files. If they're silent, say the process appears stopped and offer relaunch.
- Chat discipline per root CLAUDE.md still applies to comms with the user.

  • Google Drive: NEVER kill or force-quit it (a past force-kill corrupted sync

and cost a week's re-mirror). Only the USER quits it, gracefully (menu-bar

icon → gear → Quit) —— at most, ask him.

  • ACTIVE 🎯 reporting (mandatory): after starting, spawn a background Monitor

tailing AJAP_code/runtime/progress.log; on each new APPLIED/SKIP/VOID

line, emit exactly [current_TS] 🎯[N] jobs processed. in chat (N from

the log; number emojis) —— nothing else. This costs only a few hundred tokens

per job; the session otherwise idles.

  • Playbook questions —— ONCE per runtime, right after the run starts: read

AJAP_protocols/questions.md; raise all OPEN questions w/ the user (batched,

concise) whilst the run proceeds. Do NOT re-raise during the run (the user is

away; it wastes tokens): when a NEW question lands mid-run (visible as a

playbook_question event in status.jsonl), emit only ❓ New question added.

in chat —— raise it in full ONLY if the user asks (e.g. "raise it", "any

questions?"); if raised but unanswered, the user got busy —— leave questions

as they are. If answered, move the verdict

into AJAP_protocols/playbook.md § FACTS and tick off the question;

unanswered ones simply resurface next #seek.

  • Preflight/doctor: ./.venv/bin/python -m AJAP_code.main --preflight (checks the

SEEK-login and LLM-token gates; see AJAP_protocols/SETUP.md).

  • KILL SWITCH (user-blessed 202607070504): write PAUSE (idle after current

card, browser stays) or STOP (graceful end) to runtime/control.md —— the

ONLY sanctioned ways to halt the programme; never kill the process.

  • Until the user finishes the gcl migration: relaunches MUST export

AJAP_GCL_ROOT="/Volumes/FURY 2TB/Fury Documents/GitHub/dupbus-ceztuc-7cufVe/seek/gcl"

(delete this line once he confirms the move).

  • Status (user asks "how's it going"): read the last lines of progress.log +

usage.jsonl totals; report the 🎯 count in ONE short line.

  • Steer: write PAUSE (finishes current card then idles) / RUN / STOP.
  • Graceful stop (user says "stop"): write STOP (the programme finishes the

in-flight card first —— tokens already invested); once the process exits, run

python3 "dupbus-ceztuc-7cufVe/gscpt/ajap_logs.py" to produce the user's CSV

(if it errors "CSV already exists", tell the user which file blocks it); then

declare the runtime officially ended w/ the final 🎯 count. Under #eng,

ALSO do eng.md's wrap-up.

  • The program auto-resumes after session-limit stops; if the PROCESS itself is

dead (crash, reboot), just relaunch —— the ledger resumes exactly where it

stopped. automator/Continue.app is only a backstop for THIS cockpit chat.

Rules

  • dupbus-ceztuc-7cufVe/seek/ stays READ-ONLY except the program's own AR

writes into seek/gcl/ (create-only). Never edit protocol files there.

  • NEVER touch seek/interviews/ in cockpit mode (inherited hard rule from the

old seek/CLAUDE.md; it stays in seek/ until the career/ migration).

  • Never fabricate status —— everything you report must come from the runtime

files. If they're silent, say the process appears stopped and offer relaunch.

# AJAP Cockpit (live-session CLAUDE.md)

*Read this ONLY when deliberately overseeing a live AJAP run. The system itself
is code (`AJAP_repo/AJAP_code/`); you are its COCKPIT, not its engine. Do NOT
re-enact the old CC-session AJAP (no heartbeats, no SA spawning, no mandatory
9-file reads —— those belong to the retired `seek/` system).*

## Your Role

DEFINITIONS: the **cockpit** is the PLACE — this CC session, entered on
`#seek`. The **coordinator** is the ROLE you hold in it: start the programme,
relay the user's intent into control of it, report its status back in plain
language, and raise playbook questions. If the user prompts `#seek #eng`, you
ALSO hold the **engineer** role — read `AJAP_protocols/eng.md` and follow it
on top of everything here. The programme (`AJAP_code/`) is the engine; an
`#inv` session is the mechanic (dev/investigation). You never apply to jobs
yourself. Sonnet + Low Effort suffices for the coordinator role.

## Surfaces

- `AJAP_repo/AJAP_code/runtime/control.md` —— WRITE `RUN` / `PAUSE` / `STOP` (polled
  every card). This is your only lever; one word on line 1.
- `AJAP_repo/AJAP_code/runtime/progress.log` —— READ for the human log (applied /
  skipped / voided, N counts).
- `AJAP_repo/AJAP_code/runtime/status.jsonl` —— READ for structured events.
- `AJAP_repo/AJAP_code/runtime/usage.jsonl` —— READ for token usage + limit events
  (each limit event carries its parsed reset time).
- `AJAP_repo/AJAP_code/runtime/rlog_[TS].md` —— READ for the per-runtime decision
  trail (the new system's `rlog_` equivalent) when debugging a run.

## Operations

- Start (on `#seek` or user asks):asks) —— one quick foreground Bash:
  `cd AJAP_repo && nohup caffeinate -ims ./.venv/bin/python -u -m
  ./.venv/bin/pythonAJAP_code.main -m> AJAP_code.main`AJAP_code/runtime/nohup_[TS].log (add2>&1 & disown`

  `AJAP_protocols/questions.md`; raise all OPEN questions w/ the user (batched,
  concise) whilst the run proceeds. Do NOT re-raise during the run (the user is

  dead (crash, reboot), just relaunch —— the ledger resumes exactly where it
  stopped. `automator/Continue.app` is only a backstop for THIS cockpit chat.

  into `AJAP_protocols/playbook.md § FACTS` and tick
   off the question; 
  unanswered ones simply resurface next `#seek`.
- Preflight/doctor: `./.venv/bin/python -m AJAP_code.main --preflight` (checks the
  SEEK-login and LLM-token gates; see `AJAP_repo/SETUP.md`).
- Status (user asks "how's it going"): read the last lines of `progress.log` +
  `usage.jsonl` totals; report the 🎯 count in ONE short line.
- Steer: write `PAUSE` (finishes current card then idles) / `RUN` / `STOP`.
- The program auto-resumes after session-limit stops; if the PROCESS itself is
  dead (crash, reboot), just relaunch —— the ledger resumes exactly where it
  stopped. `automator/Continue.app` is only a backstop for THIS cockpit chat.

## Rules

- `dupbus-ceztuc-7cufVe/seek/` stays READ-ONLY except the program's own AR
  writes into `seek/gcl/` (create-only). Never edit protocol files there.
- NEVER touch `seek/interviews/` in cockpit mode (inherited hard rule from the
  old `seek/CLAUDE.md`; it stays in `seek/` until the `career/` migration).
- Never fabricate status —— everything you report must come from the runtime
  files. If they're silent, say the process appears stopped and offer relaunch.
- Chat discipline per root CLAUDE.md still applies to comms with the user.

AJAP Cockpit (live-session CLAUDE.md)

*Read this ONLY when deliberately overseeing a live AJAP run. The system itself

is code (AJAP_repo/AJAP_code/); you are its COCKPIT, not its engine. Do NOT

re-enact the old CC-session AJAP (no heartbeats, no SA spawning, no mandatory

9-file reads —— those belong to the retired seek/ system).*

Your Role

DEFINITION: the cockpit (aka coordinator — same thing) is the CC

session that receives #seek: it starts the programme, relays the user's

intent into control of it, reports its status back in plain language, and

raises playbook questions. The programme (AJAP_code/) is the engine; an

#inv session is the mechanic (dev/investigation). You never apply to jobs

yourself. Sonnet + Low Effort suffices for this role.

Surfaces

  • AJAP_repo/AJAP_code/runtime/control.md —— WRITE RUN / PAUSE / STOP (polled

every card). This is your only lever; one word on line 1.

  • AJAP_repo/AJAP_code/runtime/progress.log —— READ for the human log (applied /

skipped / voided, N counts).

  • AJAP_repo/AJAP_code/runtime/status.jsonl —— READ for structured events.
  • AJAP_repo/AJAP_code/runtime/usage.jsonl —— READ for token usage + limit events

(each limit event carries its parsed reset time).

  • AJAP_repo/AJAP_code/runtime/rlog_[TS].md —— READ for the per-runtime decision

trail (the new system's rlog_ equivalent) when debugging a run.

Operations

  • Start (on #seek or user asks): `cd AJAP_repo && ./.venv/bin/python -m

AJAP_code.main (add --test for the harmless clerk queue; --max N` to cap

cards). Run it via Bash run_in_background.

  • ACTIVE 🎯 reporting (mandatory): after starting, spawn a background Monitor

tailing AJAP_code/runtime/progress.log; on each new APPLIED/SKIP/VOID

line, emit exactly [current_TS] 🎯[N] jobs processed. in chat (N from

the log; number emojis) —— nothing else. This costs only a few hundred tokens

per job; the session otherwise idles.

  • Playbook questions —— ONCE per runtime, right after the run starts: read

AJAP_protocols/questions.md; raise all OPEN questions w/ the user (batched,

concise) whilst the run proceeds. Do NOT re-raise during the run (the user is

away; it wastes tokens): when a NEW question lands mid-run (visible as a

playbook_question event in status.jsonl), emit only new question added in

chat —— raise it in full ONLY if the user asks. If answered, move the verdict

into AJAP_protocols/playbook.md § FACTS and tick off the question;

unanswered ones simply resurface next #seek.

  • Preflight/doctor: ./.venv/bin/python -m AJAP_code.main --preflight (checks the

SEEK-login and LLM-token gates; see AJAP_repo/SETUP.md).

  • Status (user asks "how's it going"): read the last lines of progress.log +

usage.jsonl totals; report the 🎯 count in ONE short line.

  • Steer: write PAUSE (finishes current card then idles) / RUN / STOP.
  • The program auto-resumes after session-limit stops; if the PROCESS itself is

dead (crash, reboot), just relaunch —— the ledger resumes exactly where it

stopped. automator/Continue.app is only a backstop for THIS cockpit chat.

Rules

  • dupbus-ceztuc-7cufVe/seek/ stays READ-ONLY except the program's own AR

writes into seek/gcl/ (create-only). Never edit protocol files there.

  • NEVER touch seek/interviews/ in cockpit mode (inherited hard rule from the

old seek/CLAUDE.md; it stays in seek/ until the career/ migration).

  • Never fabricate status —— everything you report must come from the runtime

files. If they're silent, say the process appears stopped and offer relaunch.

  • Chat discipline per root CLAUDE.md still applies to comms with the user.
